Assumes the chapter is broken and tries to demonstrate it: refute each load-bearing claim, attack the reproduce path, find the wrong mechanism / the unreproducible step / the security hole, then report what survives. Counters correlated AI self-evaluation — MUST run on a DIFFERENT model/persona than the drafter; independence is the entire point of the gate. ([Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality](https://github.com/Pratiyush/book-java-code-quality/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/red-teamer.md) · 2 copies)
- **red-teamer**: Adversarial security analyst. Invoke to generate attack scenarios, exploit chains, penetration testing payloads, and attacker-perspective analysis. Read-only — generates attack code but does not execute it. ([r0ms3c/ai-appsec-demo](https://github.com/r0ms3c/ai-appsec-demo/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/red-teamer.md))
- **red-teamer**: Use this agent when the user wants genuine intellectual engagement, pushback on their ideas, or a thinking partner who will challenge their reasoning. This includes brainstorming sessions, philosophical discussions, technical debates, evaluating approaches or decisions, or when they explicitly ask for critical feedback. Also use when the user seems to be asking for advice where multiple perspectives and trade-offs should be considered. ([revmischa/claude-code](https://github.com/revmischa/claude-code/blob/HEAD/plugins/sjawhar-toolkit/agents/red-teamer.md))
- **red-teamer**: Generates adversarial inputs against the guardbank guardrails (prompt injection, obfuscated PII, poisoned docs, borderline topics) and reports which ones get through. Use when expanding attack coverage or probing a guardrail before an eval. ([shkroyas/AI-safety-assignment](https://github.com/shkroyas/AI-safety-assignment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/red-teamer.md))
- **red-teamer**: Adversarially attacks an artifact ABOUT TO EXECUTE — a plan before cook, a migration before it runs — hunting the failure mode the author and the happy path both missed. ([tqanh123/FinSec](https://github.com/tqanh123/FinSec/blob/HEAD/harness/plugins/hs/agents/red-teamer.md))
- **red-test-adversary**: TDD phase 2's referee. Given a new (red) test, tries to make it pass WITHOUT implementing the behaviour it claims to pin — proving whether the test is evidence or decoration. Use after writing a red test and before writing the implementation. ([pietrangelo/monitoring-agent](https://github.com/pietrangelo/monitoring-agent/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/red-test-adversary.md) · ★1)
- **red-test-evaluator**: Triages RED test writer failures with REVISE/REJECT/CONFIRM verdicts. Aligned with shared behavioral testing standard. ([navapbc/digital-service-orchestra](https://github.com/navapbc/digital-service-orchestra/blob/HEAD/plugins/dso/agents/red-test-evaluator.md) · ★6)
- **red-test-writer**: Writes behavioral RED tests for TDD workflows, rejecting change-detector patterns. ([navapbc/digital-service-orchestra](https://github.com/navapbc/digital-service-orchestra/blob/HEAD/plugins/dso/agents/red-test-writer.md) · ★6)
- **red-unit**: Spawned by implement-plan-module, Stage 2, with plan steps; or by rework-module with a rework's copy steps; or by any skill or pipeline with a reproduction brief (templates/reproducing.md). Not for direct use — it needs step context only an orchestrator has. - **reenactor**: Turns a bug report, red CI run, or production error into the smallest test that fails for the reported reason, plus the command that runs it and ranked suspect files and lines, before anyone writes a fix. Use when a bug can't yet be triggered on demand. Don't delegate when a failing test already exists, for a one-line typo, or for behavior that doesn't exist yet, which is repertoire:test-designer's job. ([stevekinney/repertoire](https://github.com/stevekinney/repertoire/blob/HEAD/agents/reenactor.md) · ★18)
- **reencuadre-vertical**: Úsalo cuando un vídeo horizontal 16:9 deba reencuadrarse a vertical 9:16 o cuadrado 1:1 y un recorte central simple cortaría al sujeto. Planifica el recorte plano a plano, sigue al sujeto y renderiza el resultado. Ejemplos: "pasa este video a vertical", "lo necesito para Reels pero esta en horizontal", "el recorte automatico corta la cara". - **reeng-frontend-uc-agent**: Genera la especificación técnica Angular to-be de un UC concreto, mapeando su lógica legacy y el contrato del backend tobe a componentes Angular, formularios, servicios y modelos. Produce un fichero [UC-CODE]_[nombre]_frontend_tobe.md. ([Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq](https://github.com/Alvaro-Dono-NTT/agentes-imq/blob/HEAD/.github/agents/reeng-frontend-uc-agent.md))
- **reengagement-agent**: Generate a personalized re-engagement message at 30, 60, or 90 days post-delivery. - **reengineering-backend-auto-agent**: Orquesta la generación de documentación de reingeniería Spring Boot para una pantalla HTML completa o para un UC concreto, centrando el diseño en un backend core reusable y sin fijar todavía la composición de frontend. - **reentrancy**: A guard that protects ONE entity (a `serviceId`/`positionId`/account) by zeroing or finalizing THAT entity's state before an external transfer does **NOT** prevent re-entry that operates on a DIFFERENT entity backed by the same shared pool. Pattern: `_withdraw`/`_claim` sets `info[id].reward = 0` ("reentrancy is now impossible") then `_transfer(receiver, amount)` to an attacker-controlled receiver ([dumbbutt0/audit-watch](https://github.com/dumbbutt0/audit-watch/blob/HEAD/agents/reentrancy.md))
- **reentrancy-agent**: Specialized agent for detecting reentrancy vulnerabilities including classic, cross-function, cross-contract, and read-only reentrancy patterns.
